1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The factorial variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 678 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 633 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_678(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 678. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^633(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any factorial module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 13. The projective resolution has length at most 437 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to factorial theory, we decompose the problem into 13 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 437. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
